### Definition — presheaf {#fc06-c02-u001}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U001` · §1 Sheaves; pp. 60–68; L1640–1648 · [not formalized]{.route-unmatched}
:::

A presheaf F on X assigns an abelian group F(U) to each open U and compatible restriction maps F(U)→F(V) for V⊆U, with F(∅)=0, identity, and composition axioms.

::: {.unit-lean}

### Convention/comparison — presheaves as contravariant functors {#fc06-c02-u002}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U002` · §1 Sheaves; pp. 60–68; L1650–1652 · after [`FC06-C02-U001`](#fc06-c02-u001) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}
:::

A presheaf is equivalently a contravariant functor Top(X)→Ab; replacing Ab gives presheaves of rings, sets, or objects of another fixed category.

::: {.unit-lean}
**Formalized.** [`Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/Presheaf.lean::TopCat.Presheaf`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/Presheaf.html#TopCat.Presheaf) — Pinned Mathlib realizes the complete source interface for this row: A presheaf is equivalently a contravariant functor Top(X)→Ab; replacing Ab gives presheaves of rings, sets, or objects of another fixed category.
:::

### Definition — sheaf {#fc06-c02-u004}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U004` · §1 Sheaves; pp. 60–68; L1658–1661 · after [`FC06-C02-U001`](#fc06-c02-u001) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}
:::

A sheaf is a presheaf satisfying locality and gluing: a section locally zero is zero, and compatible local sections on an open cover glue uniquely.

::: {.unit-lean}
**Formalized.** [`Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/Sheaf.lean::TopCat.Presheaf.IsSheaf`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/Sheaf.html#TopCat.Presheaf.IsSheaf), [`TopCat.Sheaf`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/find/?pattern=TopCat.Sheaf#doc); [`Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/SheafCondition/UniqueGluing.lean::TopCat.Presheaf.isSheaf_iff_isSheafUniqueGluing`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/SheafCondition/UniqueGluing.html#TopCat.Presheaf.isSheaf_iff_isSheafUniqueGluing) — Pinned Mathlib realizes the complete source interface for this row: A sheaf is a presheaf satisfying locality and gluing: a section locally zero is zero, and compatible local sections on an open cover glue uniquely.
:::

### Definition — stalk and germs {#fc06-c02-u008}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U008` · §1 Sheaves; pp. 60–68; L1671–1673 · after [`FC06-C02-U001`](#fc06-c02-u001), [`FC06-C01-U055`](c01.html#fc06-c01-u055) · [not formalized]{.route-unmatched}

### Definition — morphism of presheaves/sheaves {#fc06-c02-u009}

### Proposition-Definition 1.2 — associated sheaf {#fc06-c02-u012}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U012` · §1 Sheaves; pp. 60–68; L1697–1704 · after [`FC06-C02-U001`](#fc06-c02-u001), [`FC06-C02-U008`](#fc06-c02-u008) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}
:::

Every presheaf F has an associated sheaf F⁺ with a universal map F→F⁺ through which every map from F to a sheaf factors uniquely; stalks are unchanged and sheaves are fixed up to canonical isomorphism.

::: {.unit-lean}

### Construction — kernel and injective sheaf morphisms {#fc06-c02-u014}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U014` · §1 Sheaves; pp. 60–68; L1708–1710 · after [`FC06-C02-U011`](#fc06-c02-u011), [`FC06-C02-U013`](#fc06-c02-u013) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}
:::

For sheaves, the presheaf kernel is already a sheaf; φ is injective when ker φ=0, equivalently φ(U) is injective for every open U.

::: {.unit-lean}
**Formalized.** [`Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/Limits.lean`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/Limits.html); [`Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/Stalks.lean::app_injective_iff_stalkFunctor_map_injective`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/Stalks.html), `mono_iff_stalk_mono` — Pinned Mathlib creates sheaf limits from presheaf limits, so kernels are computed in the sheaf category, and its stalk/section APIs identify monomorphisms with the expected injectivity conditions.
:::

[Searched: P=db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d]{.unit-provenance}

### Construction — image and surjective sheaf morphisms {#fc06-c02-u015}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U015` · §1 Sheaves; pp. 60–68; L1712–1714 · after [`FC06-C02-U011`](#fc06-c02-u011), [`FC06-C02-U012`](#fc06-c02-u012) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}
:::

The image sheaf im φ is the sheafification of the presheaf image and embeds in G; φ is surjective when im φ=G.

::: {.unit-lean}
**Formalized.** [`Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Sites/Subsheaf.lean::Sheaf.image`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Sites/Subsheaf.html#Sheaf.image), [`Sheaf.toImage`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/find/?pattern=Sheaf.toImage#doc), `Sheaf.imageι`, `imageFactorization` — Pinned Mathlib defines the image sheaf as the sheafification of the presheaf range, gives its monomorphism into the target, and gives the epi image factorization; this is exactly Hartshorne’s image/surjective sheaf-morphism construction.
:::

[Searched: P=db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d]{.unit-provenance}

### Definition — exact sequence of sheaves {#fc06-c02-u016}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U016` · §1 Sheaves; pp. 60–68; L1716–1718 · after [`FC06-C02-U014`](#fc06-c02-u014), [`FC06-C02-U015`](#fc06-c02-u015) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}

### Comparison — exactness is stalkwise {#fc06-c02-u018}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U018` · §1 Sheaves; pp. 60–68; L1724 · after [`FC06-C02-U008`](#fc06-c02-u008), [`FC06-C02-U016`](#fc06-c02-u016) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}
:::

Surjectivity need not hold on sections over every open set, but a sheaf morphism is surjective iff all stalk maps are; more generally a sequence of sheaves is exact iff every stalk sequence is exact.

::: {.unit-lean}
**Formalized.** [`Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/LocallySurjective.lean::locally_surjective_iff_surjective_on_stalks`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/LocallySurjective.html); [`Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/Abelian.lean::TopCat.Sheaf.exact_iff_stalkFunctor_map_exact`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/Abelian.html#TopCat.Sheaf.exact_iff_stalkFunctor_map_exact) — Pinned Mathlib realizes the complete source interface for this row: Surjectivity need not hold on sections over every open set, but a sheaf morphism is surjective iff all stalk maps are; more generally a sequence of sheaves is exact iff every stalk sequence is exact.
:::

### Definition — ringed and locally ringed spaces {#fc06-c02-u028}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U028` · §2 Schemes; pp. 69–81; L1906–1916 · after [`FC06-C02-U019`](#fc06-c02-u019), [`FC06-C02-U008`](#fc06-c02-u008) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}
:::

A ringed space is (X,O_X); a morphism is (f,f^#) with f continuous and f^#:O_Y→f_*O_X. It is locally ringed when every stalk is local, and a morphism is locally ringed when all induced stalk maps are local homomorphisms.

::: {.unit-lean}
**Formalized.** [`Mathlib/AlgebraicGeometry/Scheme.lean::Scheme`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/AlgebraicGeometry/Scheme.html); [`Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/LocalPredicate.lean::LocallyRingedSpace`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Topology/Sheaves/LocalPredicate.html) — Pinned Mathlib realizes the complete source interface for this row: A ringed space is (X,O_X); a morphism is (f,f^#) with f continuous and f^#:O_Y→f_*O_X. It is locally ringed when every stalk is local, and a morphism is locally ringed when all induced stalk maps are local homomorphisms.
:::

[Searched: P=db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d]{.unit-provenance}

### Proposition 2.5 — standard affine cover of Proj {#fc06-c02-u043}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U043` · §2 Schemes; pp. 69–81; L1989–2001 · after [`FC06-C02-U042`](#fc06-c02-u042), [`FC06-C02-U031`](#fc06-c02-u031), [`FC06-C02-U027`](#fc06-c02-u027) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}
:::

### Definition — schemes over a base {#fc06-c02-u045}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U045` · §2 Schemes; pp. 69–81; L2015 · after [`FC06-C02-U031`](#fc06-c02-u031) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}
:::

A scheme over S is a scheme X with a morphism X→S; an S-morphism commutes with structure maps. Sch(S) denotes their category.

::: {.unit-lean}
**Formalized.** [`Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Comma/Over/Basic.lean::CategoryTheory.Over`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Comma/Over/Basic.html#CategoryTheory.Over) — The generic over-category applied to schemes is exactly the category of schemes equipped with a structure morphism to the fixed base and morphisms commuting with those maps.
:::

[Searched: P=db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d]{.unit-provenance}

### Theorem 4.7 — valuative criterion of properness {#fc06-c02-u089}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U089` · §4 Separated and Proper Morphisms; pp. 95–107; L2486–2510 · after [`FC06-C02-U087`](#fc06-c02-u087), [`FC06-C02-U083`](#fc06-c02-u083), [`FC06-C02-U052`](#fc06-c02-u052) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}
:::

### Definition — quasi-coherent and coherent sheaves {#fc06-c02-u111}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U111` · §5 Sheaves of Modules; pp. 108–128; L2724 · after [`FC06-C02-U108`](#fc06-c02-u108), [`FC06-C02-U052`](#fc06-c02-u052) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}
:::

An O_X-module F is quasi-coherent if locally on affine U_i=Spec A_i it is M_i~; it is coherent if the M_i can be finitely generated (normally used on noetherian schemes).

::: {.unit-lean}
**Formalized.** [`Mathlib/Algebra/Category/ModuleCat/Sheaf/Quasicoherent.lean::SheafOfModules.IsQuasicoherent`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Algebra/Category/ModuleCat/Sheaf/Quasicoherent.html#SheafOfModules.IsQuasicoherent); [`Mathlib/Algebra/Category/ModuleCat/Sheaf/Generators.lean::SheafOfModules.IsFiniteType`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Algebra/Category/ModuleCat/Sheaf/Generators.html#SheafOfModules.IsFiniteType); [`Mathlib/AlgebraicGeometry/Modules/Tilde.lean::Scheme.Modules.isQuasicoherent_iff_isIso_fromTildeΓ`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/AlgebraicGeometry/Modules/Tilde.html) — Pinned Mathlib formalizes quasi-coherence by local presentations and proves the affine tilde/global-sections characterization; its finite-type module-sheaf predicate is local finite generation. Their conjunction realizes Hartshorne’s quasi-coherent/coherent definition used here.
:::

[Searched: P=db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d]{.unit-provenance}

### Corollary 5.10 — affine ideals classify closed subschemes {#fc06-c02-u127}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U127` · §5 Sheaves of Modules; pp. 108–128; L2808–2812 · after [`FC06-C02-U061`](#fc06-c02-u061), [`FC06-C02-U126`](#fc06-c02-u126), [`FC06-C02-U119`](#fc06-c02-u119) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}

### Definition — generated by global sections {#fc06-c02-u142}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U142` · §5 Sheaves of Modules; pp. 108–128; L2892–2894 · after [`FC06-C02-U003`](#fc06-c02-u003), [`FC06-C02-U100`](#fc06-c02-u100) · [in Mathlib]{.route-mathlib}
:::

F is generated by global sections when some family of global sections generates every stalk; equivalently F is a quotient of a free sheaf.

::: {.unit-lean}
**Formalized.** [`Mathlib/Algebra/Category/ModuleCat/Sheaf/Generators.lean::SheafOfModules.GeneratingSections`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Algebra/Category/ModuleCat/Sheaf/Generators.html#SheafOfModules.GeneratingSections), `SheafOfModules.GeneratingSections.π`, [`SheafOfModules.freeHomEquiv`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/find/?pattern=SheafOfModules.freeHomEquiv#doc) — A pinned GeneratingSections object is a family of global sections whose associated map from a free sheaf is epi; this is exactly the source equivalence between stalkwise generation by global sections and being a quotient of a free sheaf.
:::

### Proposition 7.7 — sections and effective divisors in a linear class {#fc06-c02-u218}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U218` · §7 Projective Morphisms; pp. 149–171; L3640–3650 · after [`FC06-C02-U191`](#fc06-c02-u191), [`FC06-C02-U198`](#fc06-c02-u198), [`FC06-C02-U091`](#fc06-c02-u091) · [not formalized]{.route-unmatched}
:::

For nonsingular projective X and L≅L(D_0): every nonzero section s has an effective zero divisor (s)_0∼D_0; every effective divisor linearly equivalent to D_0 arises this way; two sections give the same zero divisor iff they differ by a scalar in k^*.

### Theorem 8.21A — Cohen–Macaulay and regular-sequence facts {#fc06-c02-u289}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U289` · §8 Differentials; pp. 172–189; L4216–4220 · after [`FC06-C02-U052`](#fc06-c02-u052), [`FC06-C01-U094`](c01.html#fc06-c01-u094) · [not formalized]{.route-unmatched}
:::

For local noetherian A: regular implies Cohen–Macaulay; CM localizes; in a CM ring x_1,…,x_r is regular iff quotient dimension drops by r; quotient by a regular sequence is CM; and for I=(x_i), gr_I A≅(A/I)[t_1,…,t_r], so I/I² is free and Sym^n(I/I²)≅I^n/I^{n+1}.

::: {.unit-lean}
**Not formalized.** none; no complete checked declaration under strict bundle semantics — As of 2026-09-06, no checked project/P/H/open/external/indexed Lean declaration realizes the whole source row: For local noetherian A: regular implies Cohen–Macaulay; CM localizes; in a CM ring x_1,…,x_r is regular iff quotient dimension drops by r; quotient by a regular sequence is CM; and for I=(x_i), gr_I A≅(A/I)[t_1,…,t_r], so I/I² is free and Sym^n(I/I²)≅I^n/I^{n+1}. ### Definition — noetherian formal scheme, morphism, coherent sheaf {#fc06-c02-u313}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U313` · §9 Formal Schemes; pp. 190–200; L4481 · after [`FC06-C02-U310`](#fc06-c02-u310), [`FC06-C02-U312`](#fc06-c02-u312), [`FC06-C02-U028`](#fc06-c02-u028) · [not formalized]{.route-unmatched}
:::

A noetherian formal scheme is a locally ringed space with a finite open cover by completions of noetherian schemes along closed subschemes; morphisms are locally-ringed-space morphisms, and coherent modules are locally completions of coherent modules on such algebraizations.

::: {.unit-lean}
**Not formalized.** none; no complete checked declaration under strict bundle semantics — As of 2026-09-06, no checked project/P/H/open/external/indexed Lean declaration realizes the whole source row: A noetherian formal scheme is a locally ringed space with a finite open cover by completions of noetherian schemes along closed subschemes; morphisms are locally-ringed-space morphisms, and coherent modules are locally completions of coherent modules on such algebraizations. ### Proposition 9.5 — properties of ideals of definition {#fc06-c02-u320}

::: {.unit-meta}
`FC06-C02-U320` · §9 Formal Schemes; pp. 190–200; L4525–4539 · after [`FC06-C02-U319`](#fc06-c02-u319), [`FC06-C02-U048`](#fc06-c02-u048) · [not formalized]{.route-unmatched}
:::
